# Chinese frontier-AI institutions, third collection: candidate pack

Generated from `pack.json` by `node research/china-labs-3/tools.mjs render`; do not edit by hand. A candidate pack outside application data: nothing here is editorially accepted. See [README.md](README.md) for method and limits and [scope.md](scope.md) for the choice of institutions.

212 records, 112 sources (38 in Chinese), 102 proposed entities, 20 existing entities reused.

Chinese passages are quoted in the original. Their English renderings are machine translations; no human translation verification is claimed. "Fixed" marks an arXiv version, a Git or Hugging Face commit or a filing; "mutable" marks a page read as it stood on retrieval.

### Passages and qualifications

- **`c3-01ai-yi-licence-v1`** zero-one-ai → `published` → Yi Series Models License Agreement, Version 1.0. The licence is described as the text describes itself; no judgement is made whether it meets any definition of open source. Its definition clause calls the Yi Series Models "opensource models". Coordinator recheck of the repository history: the file cited (e055d54, 1 November 2023) was reformatted the same day (a7c6fc5) and its contact address changed on 2 November (ce65074); on 4 November 2023 the sentence limiting use to non-commercial purposes was removed while the file still read Version 1.0 (18156fa), and the file was relabelled Version 2.0, dated 4 November 2023, later that day (c8651b5).
  - supports, primary: “Yi Series Models License Agreement Version [1.0] Date of Release [November 1, 2023]” (01.AI (01-ai, Hugging Face), Heading)
- **`c3-01ai-yi-licence-v1-derivatives`** yi-licence-v1-0 → `states_provision` → “Derivatives include models trained on synthetic data generated from Yi models”. What the definition clause states; enforcement is not recorded. Coordinator recheck of the repository history: the file cited (e055d54, 1 November 2023) was reformatted the same day (a7c6fc5) and its contact address changed on 2 November (ce65074); on 4 November 2023 the sentence limiting use to non-commercial purposes was removed while the file still read Version 1.0 (18156fa), and the file was relabelled Version 2.0, dated 4 November 2023, later that day (c8651b5).
  - supports, primary: “generating synthetic data based on Yi Series Models to train other models” (01.AI (01-ai, Hugging Face), Section 1, definition of "Derivatives")
- **`c3-01ai-yi-licence-v1-laws`** yi-licence-v1-0 → `states_provision` → “"Laws and Regulations" defined as those of mainland China; no military or unlawful use”. What the licence text states; it also refers to the applicable legal requirements of other countries and regions. Enforcement is not recorded. Coordinator recheck of the repository history: the file cited (e055d54, 1 November 2023) was reformatted the same day (a7c6fc5) and its contact address changed on 2 November (ce65074); on 4 November 2023 the sentence limiting use to non-commercial purposes was removed while the file still read Version 1.0 (18156fa), and the file was relabelled Version 2.0, dated 4 November 2023, later that day (c8651b5).
  - supports, primary: “refers to the laws and administrative regulations of the mainland of the People's Republic of China” (01.AI (01-ai, Hugging Face), Section 1, definition of "Laws and Regulations")
  - supports, primary: “You shall not, for military or unlawful purposes” (01.AI (01-ai, Hugging Face), Section 2, restriction 2)
- **`c3-01ai-yi-licence-v1-noncommercial`** yi-licence-v1-0 → `states_provision` → “Use for non-commercial purposes only; commercial use requires the licensor's prior written authorisation”. What the licence text states; later versions changed the commercial route (see version 2.1). Enforcement is not recorded. Coordinator recheck of the repository history: the file cited (e055d54, 1 November 2023) was reformatted the same day (a7c6fc5) and its contact address changed on 2 November (ce65074); on 4 November 2023 the sentence limiting use to non-commercial purposes was removed while the file still read Version 1.0 (18156fa), and the file was relabelled Version 2.0, dated 4 November 2023, later that day (c8651b5).
  - supports, primary: “You may only use the Yi Series Models for non-Commercial Purposes” (01.AI (01-ai, Hugging Face), Section 2, "License and License Restrictions")
  - supports, primary: “you should contact the Licensor in advance as specified in Section 7 of this Agreement” (01.AI (01-ai, Hugging Face), Section 2, final paragraph)
- **`c3-01ai-yi-34b-card`** zero-one-ai → `published` → Yi-34B model card. The card's description is the developer's own; benchmark tables are out of scope. "Trained from scratch" is the card's wording; see the separate record on the card's later statement on Llama.
  - supports, primary: “The first public release contains two bilingual(English/Chinese) base models with the parameter sizes of 6B and 34B.” (01.AI (01-ai, Hugging Face), README, "Introduction")
  - supports, primary: “2023/11/02**: The base model of `Yi-6B` and `Yi-34B`.” (01.AI (01-ai, Hugging Face), README, "News")
  - context, primary: “2023.11 Yi-34B 开源发布” — machine translation: “2023.11 Yi-34B open-source release” (01.AI (零一万物), 公司介绍, timeline entry 2023.11)
- **`c3-01ai-legal-form-licensor`** zero-one-ai → `has_legal_form` → “北京零一万物信息技术有限公司 (Beijing Lingyiwanwu Information Technology Co., Ltd), a limited liability company, named as licensor of the Yi models”. The licence names the party granting rights in the Yi models; it is not a registry extract and does not state the company's registration or that it is 01.AI's only entity. The AIIA signatory list (c2-governance-aiia-aisc-signatory-zero-one-ai) and the CAC filing list retrieved for this pack's governance group name a differently worded company, 北京零一万物科技有限公司; no relation between the two names is inferred. 有限公司 is rendered "limited liability company".
  - supports, primary: ““Licensor” refers to Beijing Lingyiwanwu Information Technology Co., Ltd.” (01.AI (01-ai, Hugging Face), Section 1 (English), definition of "Licensor")
  - supports, primary: ““许可方”是指北京零一万物信息技术有限公司。” — machine translation: “"Licensor" means Beijing Lingyiwanwu Information Technology Co., Ltd.” (01.AI (01-ai, Hugging Face), 第1条 (Chinese), 定义)
  - supports, primary: ““许可方”是指【北京零一万物信息技术有限公司】” — machine translation: “"Licensor" means [Beijing Lingyiwanwu Information Technology Co., Ltd.]” (01.AI (01-ai, Hugging Face), Section 1 (Chinese), definition, version 1.0)
- **`c3-01ai-yi-licence-v21`** zero-one-ai → `published` → Yi Series Models Community License Agreement, Version 2.1. The licence is described as the text describes itself. The agreement is the third text in the file's history (versions 1.0 of 1 November and 2.0 of 4 November 2023 preceded it); the predecessor named by the update notice is not stated, so no supersession record is drafted.
  - supports, primary: “Yi Series Models Community License Agreement Version: 2.1 Date of Release: November 23, 2023” (01.AI (01-ai, Hugging Face), Heading)
  - supports, primary: “2023-11-23: The Yi Series Models Community License Agreement is updated to v2.1.” (01.AI (01-ai, Hugging Face), README, "News")
- **`c3-01ai-yi-licence-v21-registration`** yi-community-licence-v2-1 → `states_provision` → “Commercial use licensed by submitting a registration form”. What the licence text states; version 1.0 had required prior written authorisation instead. 登记 is rendered "registration". Whether registrations were processed is not recorded.
  - supports, primary: “please refer to the Registration Form of Yi Series Models for Commercial Purposes” (01.AI (01-ai, Hugging Face), Section 2, final paragraph)
  - supports, primary: “to complete the registration and obtain the license for Commercial Purposes.” (01.AI (01-ai, Hugging Face), Section 2, final paragraph)
- **`c3-01ai-yi-llama-statement`** zero-one-ai → `published` → Yi-34B model card, "Yi's relation with Llama". What 01.AI's card states about its own models; it does not establish the technical relation between Yi and Llama, which is not assessed here. The background of the statement is not recorded.
  - supports, primary: “The Yi series models adopt the same model architecture as Llama but are **NOT** derivatives of Llama.” (01.AI (01-ai, Hugging Face), README, "Yi's relation with Llama", TL;DR)
- **`c3-01ai-yi-report`** zero-one-ai → `published` → Yi: Open Foundation Models by 01.AI. The report is the developer's own description; performance claims in it are out of scope. The author list names 01.AI staff in alphabetical order; no person in it is identified with an existing dataset entity.
  - supports, primary: “Yi: Open Foundation Models by 01.AI 01.AI” (arXiv, Title page)
  - supports, primary: “The Yi model family is based on 6B and 34B pretrained language models” (arXiv, arXiv abstract page, abstract)

### Passages and qualifications

- **`c3-iflytek-founding-1999`** iflytek → `founding_stated` → “Predecessor 安徽中科大讯飞信息科技有限公司 established on 30 December 1999”. The founding of the predecessor limited company, as the issuer’s filing states it. The prospectus gives 26 April 2007 as the establishment date (设立日期) of the joint-stock company.
  - supports, primary: “本公司前身安徽中科大讯飞信息科技有限公司成立于 1999 年 12 月 30 日” — machine translation: “The company’s predecessor, Anhui USTC iFlytek Information Technology Co., Ltd., was established on 30 December 1999” (Anhui USTC iFlytek Co., Ltd. (via cninfo), Section 2, Overview, 1. Issuer profile, “2、设立情况”)
- **`c3-iflytek-legal-form-2008`** iflytek → `has_legal_form` → “安徽科大讯飞信息科技股份有限公司 (ANHUI USTC iFLYTEK CO.,LTD.), a joint-stock company limited by shares converted as a whole from a limited liability company on 26 April 2007”. 整体变更 is rendered “converted as a whole”; 工商变更登记 is a change registration (登记), not a filing (备案). The registration number in the passage is not recorded.
  - supports, primary: “公司名称：安徽科大讯飞信息科技股份有限公司 英文名称：ANHUI USTC iFLYTEK CO.,LTD.” — machine translation: “Company name: Anhui USTC iFlytek Information Technology Co., Ltd. (joint-stock); English name: ANHUI USTC iFLYTEK CO.,LTD.” (Anhui USTC iFlytek Co., Ltd. (via cninfo), Section 2, Overview, 1. Issuer profile)
  - supports, primary: “2007 年 4 月 26 日整体变更为安徽科大讯飞信息科技股份有限公司，并在安徽省 工商行政管理局办理了工商变更登记” — machine translation: “on 26 April 2007 it was converted as a whole into Anhui USTC iFlytek Information Technology Co., Ltd. (joint-stock) and completed the change registration with the Anhui Provincial Administration for Industry and Commerce” (Anhui USTC iFlytek Co., Ltd. (via cninfo), Section 2, Overview, “2、设立情况”)
- **`c3-iflytek-liu-qingfeng-president-2008`** liu-qingfeng → `held_role` → iFlytek / 科大讯飞. 总裁 is rendered “president”.
  - supports, primary: “刘庆峰先生，董事、总裁，中国国籍” — machine translation: “Mr. Liu Qingfeng, director and president, Chinese nationality” (Anhui USTC iFlytek Co., Ltd. (via cninfo), Section 8, Directors, supervisors and senior management, (1) Directors)
- **`c3-iflytek-wang-renhua-chair-2008`** wang-renhua → `held_role` → iFlytek / 科大讯飞. Public roles as the filing states them. His academic positions in the same passage are not recorded.
  - supports, primary: “王仁华先生，董事长、讯飞研究院院长” — machine translation: “Mr. Wang Renhua, chairman of the board and head of the iFlytek Research Institute” (Anhui USTC iFlytek Co., Ltd. (via cninfo), Section 8, (1) Directors)
  - supports, primary: “4、法定代表人：王仁华” — machine translation: “4. Legal representative: Wang Renhua” (Anhui USTC iFlytek Co., Ltd. (via cninfo), Section 5, Basic information)
- **`c3-iflytek-liu-qingfeng-chair-2009`** liu-qingfeng → `held_role` → iFlytek / 科大讯飞. Read from the layout rendering of the table on p. 73, where the text layer separates names from dates. The start date of a term in the table may refer to his service as director or chairman; the table does not distinguish.
  - supports, primary: “刘庆峰 男 50 董事长 现任 … 任期起 始日期 任期终 止日期 2009 年 04 月 13 日” — machine translation: “Liu Qingfeng, male, 50, chairman, in office … term start date, term end date: 13 April 2009” (iFlytek Co., Ltd. (via cninfo), Section 4, V. Directors, supervisors and senior management, 1. Basic information (table, p. 73))
- **`c3-iflytek-investor-china-mobile`** china-mobile → `participated_in_round` → iFlytek / 科大讯飞. The subscriber named is 中国移动通信有限公司 (China Mobile Communication Co., Ltd.). The dataset entity china-mobile carries 中国移动通信集团有限公司, a different legal name; the cited passage states no parent link, and none is inferred. Held until the subscriber has its own entity or the link is documented. No amount is recorded; no influence is inferred from the shareholding.
  - supports, primary: “中国移动通信有限公司作为战略投资者，在 2013 年非公开发行中认购 70,273,975 股公司股份，锁定期 36 个月” — machine translation: “China Mobile Communication Co., Ltd., as a strategic investor, subscribed for 70,273,975 shares of the company in the 2013 non-public offering, with a 36-month lock-up period” (iFlytek Co., Ltd. (via cninfo), Section 7, Top-ten shareholders, note 3)
- **`c3-sensetime-founding-2014`** sensetime → `founding_stated` → “SenseTime Group Inc. incorporated as an exempted company with limited liability in the Cayman Islands on 15 October 2014”. Incorporation of the listed holding company, not a date for the start of research work. The prospectus says Prof. Tang Xiao’ou joined the group in October 2014.
  - supports, primary: “Our Company was incorporated as an exempted company with limited liability in the Cayman Islands on October 15, 2014” (SenseTime Group Inc. (via HKEXnews), History and Corporate Structure, Overview (p. 199))
  - context, primary: “OUR KEY MILESTONES The following is a summary of our Group’s key business development milestones: Date Event 2014 . . . . . . . . Our Company was incorporated” (SenseTime Group Inc. (via HKEXnews), History and Corporate Structure, “Our Key Milestones”)
- **`c3-sensetime-pre-ipo-rounds`** sensetime → `announced_funding` → “Twelve rounds of Pre-IPO Investments (Series A-1 to Series D+), between 26 May 2015 and 29 June 2021”. A filing statement of completed rounds. Round-level amounts and valuations in the prospectus table are not recorded; no round total is attributed to any investor.
  - supports, primary: “Between May 26, 2015 and June 29, 2021, we conducted twelve rounds of Pre-IPO Investments.” (SenseTime Group Inc. (via HKEXnews), History and Corporate Structure, Major Shareholding Changes, item 4)
  - supports, primary: “We have received twelve rounds of Pre-IPO Investments since our establishment and our Pre-IPO Investors were issued Preferred Shares in our Company” (SenseTime Group Inc. (via HKEXnews), History and Corporate Structure, Pre-IPO Investments, 1. Overview)
- **`c3-sensetime-xu-bing-director-2015-2025`** xu-bing → `held_role` → SenseTime / 商汤科技. Board roles only; the announcement records his confirmation of no disagreement with the Board, which is not recorded as a reason. No later role is inferred.
  - supports, primary: “is our co-founder, executive Director and Board secretary. He was appointed as a Director on December 15, 2015 and re-designated as an executive Director on August 23, 2021.” (SenseTime Group Inc. (via HKEXnews), Directors and Senior Management, Executive Directors)
  - supports, primary: “Mr. Xu Bing has stepped down as an executive Director and the secretary of the Board following the conclusion of the Annual General Meeting” (SenseTime Group Inc. (via HKEXnews), Section 4, Stepping down of an executive Director)
- **`c3-sensetime-tang-director-2021-2023`** tang-xiaoou → `held_role` → SenseTime / 商汤科技. Only the documented public role and dates are recorded. The announcement’s tribute wording and the circumstances it mentions are not recorded.
  - supports, primary: “is our founder and was appointed as our executive Director on August 23, 2021.” (SenseTime Group Inc. (via HKEXnews), Directors and Senior Management, Executive Directors)
  - supports, primary: “the controlling shareholder of the Company and an executive director of the Board, has passed away on December 15, 2023” (SenseTime Group Inc. (via HKEXnews), First paragraph)
- **`c3-sensetime-investor-idg`** idg-capital → `participated_in_round` → SenseTime / 商汤科技. The funds, not a parent, are the investing entities; linking them to the idg-capital entity rests on the fund names. The capitalisation table shows holdings at the prospectus date, read from the layout rendering of p. 208; it does not show whether the shares were subscribed in the Series A-1 round or acquired by transfer. No round amount is attributed to the funds. The prospectus does not name IDG Capital; the funds are mapped to idg-capital by the IDG name prefix.
  - supports, primary: “Each of IDG China Venture Capital Fund IV, L.P. (the “IDG Main Fund”) and IDG China IV Investors L.P. (the “IDG Side Fund”) is an exempted limited partnership” (SenseTime Group Inc. (via HKEXnews), History and Corporate Structure, 5. Information relating to our principal Pre-IPO Investors, (d))
  - supports, primary: “IDG China IV Investors L.P. (Note 3) . . . . . . . — 51,080,000” (SenseTime Group Inc. (via HKEXnews), Capitalization of our Company table (p. 208), Series A-1 Preferred Shares column)
- **`c3-sensetime-investor-iflytek`** iflytek → `participated_in_round` → SenseTime / 商汤科技. The table shows a holding of Series A-1 Preferred Shares, read from the layout rendering of p. 208 (the text layer separates the column header from the row). Participation in the Series A-1 round is inferred from the share class; the table does not show whether the shares were subscribed or acquired by transfer. Iflytek Co., Ltd. is matched to iFlytek by name. No amount is attributed to iFlytek and no influence is inferred.
  - supports, primary: “Iflytek Co., Ltd. . . — 25,000,000” (SenseTime Group Inc. (via HKEXnews), Capitalization of our Company table (p. 208), row “Iflytek Co., Ltd.”, Series A-1 Preferred Shares column)
- **`c3-sensetime-investor-softbank`** softbank-group → `participated_in_round` → SenseTime / 商汤科技. The investing entity is SVF Sense. The filing traces the ownership of SVF Sense to SVF Holdings (UK) LLP, and states that SoftBank Group ultimately wholly owns the general partner (and the manager), not SVF Sense itself. The subject softbank-group stands for that chain only through the general partner and manager; whether a fund or vehicle entity should be the subject is held for the maintainer. ### Passages and qualifications

- **`c3-baai-founding`** baai → `founding_stated` → “Established on 14 November 2018 (as BAAI states it)”. The sponsoring bodies are recorded in BAAI's own words (指导和支持, 推动成立: 'guidance and support', 'promoted the establishment'); no direction, control or ownership is inferred from them.   - supports, primary: “we identify critical risks in seven areas: cyber offense, biological and chemical risks, persuasion and manipulation, uncontrolled autonomous AI R&D, strategic deception and scheming, self-replication, and collusion” (arXiv, Abstract)
- **`c3-shlab-frmf-practice-zhou-bowen`** zhou-bowen → `authored` → Frontier AI Risk Management Framework in Practice: A Risk Analysis Technical Report. The report defines Scientific Director as responsible for the strategic and organisational aspects of the project. The identification with the existing person record rests on the name in a report credited to Shanghai AI Laboratory, where he is recorded as director from April 2024 (lg-gc-zhou-bowen-shanghai-ai-lab-2024).
  - supports, primary: “Scientific Director: Bowen Zhou” (arXiv, Authorship section)
- **`c3-shlab-frmf-practice-zones`** sail-frmf-in-practice-report → `states_provision` → “Risk zones: green (routine deployment with monitoring), yellow (strengthened mitigations, controlled deployment), red (suspension of development and/or deployment)”. Zone definitions as the report states them; it does not establish that any developer has adopted them.
  - supports, primary: “yellow (requiring strengthened mitigations and controlled deployment), and red (necessitating suspension of development and/or deployment)” (arXiv, Abstract)
- **`c3-shlab-intern-s1-licence`** shanghai-ai-lab → `published` → Intern-S1 licence (Apache License 2.0). The file is the standard Apache 2.0 text with no filled-in copyright line; it is recorded as the repository's licence statement for the weights in that repository only.
  - supports, primary: “Apache License Version 2.0, January 2004” (InternLM (Hugging Face), Heading)
  - supports, primary: “license: apache-2.0” (InternLM (Hugging Face), YAML front matter)
- **`c3-shlab-safework-r1-report`** shanghai-ai-lab → `published` → SafeWork-R1: Coevolving Safety and Intelligence under the AI-45° Law. The report's statements about safety and capability results are its own and are out of scope; the base-model relation is recorded as the report states it.
  - supports, primary: “SafeWork-R1: Coevolving Safety and Intelligence under the AI-45◦ Law Shanghai Artificial Intelligence Laboratory” (arXiv, Title page)
  - context, primary: “over its base model Qwen2.5-VL-72B” (arXiv, Abstract)
- **`c3-shlab-safework-r1-safeladder`** safework-r1-report → `states_provision` → “SafeLadder: large-scale, progressive, safety-oriented reinforcement-learning post-training with multi-principled verifiers”. A description of the developer's method in its own report, not an independent evaluation of its effect.
  - supports, primary: “SafeLadder framework, which incorporates large-scale, progressive, safety-oriented reinforcement learning post-training, supported by a suite of multi-principled verifiers” (arXiv, Abstract)
- **`c3-shlab-safework-r1-zhou-bowen`** zhou-bowen → `authored` → SafeWork-R1: Coevolving Safety and Intelligence under the AI-45° Law. The report defines Scientific Director as responsible for the strategic and organisational aspects of the project. The identification with the existing person record rests on the name in a report credited to Shanghai AI Laboratory, where he is recorded as director from April 2024 (lg-gc-zhou-bowen-shanghai-ai-lab-2024).
  - supports, primary: “Scientific Director: Bowen Zhou” (arXiv, Authorship section)
- **`c3-shlab-intern-s1-card`** shanghai-ai-lab → `published` → Intern-S1 model card. The card's comparative performance claims are out of scope. Attribution to Shanghai AI Laboratory rests on the internlm organisation and on the technical report's credit (c3-shlab-intern-s1-report).
  - supports, primary: “Built upon a 235B MoE language model and a 6B Vision encoder” (InternLM (Hugging Face), Introduction)
- **`c3-shlab-intern-s1-report`** shanghai-ai-lab → `published` → Intern-S1: A Scientific Multimodal Foundation Model. Credit as printed on the title page. Performance claims are out of scope.
  - supports, primary: “Intern-S1 Team, Shanghai AI Laboratory” (arXiv, Title page)

- **`c3-governance-tc260-asgf-v1-developer-ethics-review`** tc260-asgf-v1-0 → `states_provision` → “Developers to apply measures such as internal discussion, expert review and science-and-technology ethics review at key stages”. What the cited edition says, as guidance in a technical document of TC260 (a national standardisation technical committee); it is not evidence that any developer adopted, implemented or was required to follow it.
  - supports, primary: “研发者应在需求分析、项目立项、模型设计开发、训练数据选用等 关键环节 … 组织专家评议、科技伦理审查、听取公众意见” — machine translation: “Developers should, at key stages such as requirements analysis, project approval, model design and development and training-data selection … take measures such as holding internal discussions, organising expert review, conducting science-and-technology ethics review and hearing public opinion” (全国网络安全标准化技术委员会, §6.1(a) 模型算法研发者安全开发指引)
- **`c3-governance-tc260-asgf-v1-loss-of-control-risk`** tc260-asgf-v1-0 → `states_provision` → “Risk of future loss of control: AI acquiring external resources, self-replicating and seeking external power”. What the cited edition says, as guidance in a technical document of TC260 (a national standardisation technical committee); it is not evidence that any developer adopted, implemented or was required to follow it.
  - supports, primary: “不排除人工 智能自主获取外部资源、自我复制，产生自我意识，寻求外部权力，带来谋求 与人类争夺控制权的风险” — machine translation: “it is not ruled out that AI may autonomously acquire external resources, self-replicate, develop self-awareness and seek external power, bringing the risk of seeking to compete with humans for control” (全国网络安全标准化技术委员会, §3.2 (c) 未来脱离控制的风险)
- **`c3-governance-tc260-asgf-v1-open-source-supply-chain`** tc260-asgf-v1-0 → `states_provision` → “Promote sharing of AI knowledge and open-sourcing of AI technology for supply-chain security”. - **`c3-governance-tc260-asgf-v3-regulatory-sandbox`** tc260-asgf-v3-0 → `states_provision` → “Sandbox regulation rules combining sector classification with risk grading”. What the cited edition says, as guidance in a technical document of TC260 (a national standardisation technical committee); it is not evidence that any developer adopted, implemented or was required to follow it.
  - supports, primary: “建立行业分类与风险分级相结合的沙箱监管规则。明确沙箱监管适 用对象、测试范围、权责关系和退出条件” — machine translation: “Establish sandbox regulatory rules combining sector classification with risk grading. Clarify the applicable objects of sandbox regulation, the test scope, the allocation of powers and responsibilities, and exit conditions” (全国网络安全标准化技术委员会, §4.3.1)
- **`c3-governance-tc260-asgf-v3-updates-v2`** tc260-asgf-v3-0 → `supersedes_document` → AI Safety Governance Framework 2.0 (人工智能安全治理框架2.0). The text names both earlier editions as its basis and describes updating them; it does not state that 2.0 or 1.0 is withdrawn. Recorded against 2.0, the immediate predecessor; the same passage also names 1.0. Whether supersedes_document fits is a reviewer decision.
  - supports, primary: “在《人工智能安全治理框架 1.0》 （2024年）、《人工智能安全治理框架2.0》 （2025年）基础上，研究编制《人 工智能安全治理框架3.0》” — machine translation: “on the basis of the AI Safety Governance Framework 1.0 (2024) and the AI Safety Governance Framework 2.0 (2025), researched and compiled the AI Safety Governance Framework 3.0” (全国网络安全标准化技术委员会, Preface)
- **`c3-governance-tc260-asgf-v3-upstream-flaw-assessment`** tc260-asgf-v3-0 → `states_provision` → “Developers building on foundation models to assess security flaws of the model and upstream components”. What the cited edition says, as guidance in a technical document of TC260 (a national standardisation technical committee); it is not evidence that any developer adopted, implemented or was required to follow it.
  - supports, primary: “基于基础大模型进行二次开发的，开展模型及上游组件安全缺陷 评估，防范上游缺陷向下游模型和应用扩散” — machine translation: “Those carrying out secondary development based on foundation large models assess security flaws of the model and upstream components, to prevent upstream flaws spreading to downstream models and applications” (全国网络安全标准化技术委员会, §5.1.13)

## What each drafting pass searched

- **StepFun (阶跃星辰) and 01.AI (零一万物).** Searched (all retrieval direct, sequential, 29 October 2026): the arXiv API for "StepFun" (12 papers listed) and the abstract pages and v1 PDFs of Step-Video-T2V (2502.10248), Step-Audio (2502.11946), Step1X-Edit (2504.17761), Step-Audio 2 (2507.16632), Step-3 (2507.19427) and NextStep-1 (2508.10711); the Hugging Face model listing of stepfun-ai (55 repositories) and the commit lists and pinned README/LICENSE files of step3, Step-3.5-Flash, Step-5-Preview, stepvideo-t2v and Step-Audio-Chat; stepfun.com (https://www.stepfun.com/ and https://www.stepfun.com/company both return HTTP 200 with a 2,262-byte JavaScript shell and no text; the newsroom posts and footer were read in the site's content-hashed build file https://www.stepfun.com/assets/index-4zBpLKmx.js, HTTP 200) and one static blog page (static.stepfun.com/blog/step-3.5-flash/, retrieved, not cited); the 36Kr republication of QbitAI of 27 January 2026 already in the dataset. For 01.AI: the abstract pages and v1 PDFs of the Yi report (2403.04652) and the Yi-Lightning report (2412.01253); the Hugging Face listing of 01-ai (28 repositories), commit lists of Yi-34B, Yi-1.5-34B and Yi-Coder-9B, and a blob-less clone of the Yi-34B Hugging Face repository and of github.com/01-ai/Yi (scratch directory) for LICENSE and README history; lingyiwanwu.com (home, about, newsroom; https://www.lingyiwanwu.com/yi-license, named in the v2.1 licence as the home of the commercial licence agreement, now serves the homepage, HTTP 200, so that agreement was not retrieved), www.01.ai (English home) and two WeChat posts linked from the 01.AI newsroom. A few Hugging Face API metadata lookups (model info, file trees at a commit) were made with plain curl for discovery only and are not cited. No retrieval was refused with an HTTP error; the failures are content failures: the stepfun.com JavaScript shell (above), the removed yi-license page (above), and the stepvideo-t2v README at commit fe3a312810e4 (2025-02-14), which holds only a licence tag. Web.archive.org was not tried (refuses connections here); the cross-family checker may wish to open archive copies of https://www.stepfun.com/, https://www.stepfun.com/company and https://www.lingyiwanwu.com/yi-license. Left out: (1) a StepFun founding statement in its own words exists only in the meta description of the stepfun.com HTML shell ("阶跃星辰于2023年4月成立"), which the tool's text layer excludes, so it was not drafted; the existing reporting-based record lg-cn-stepfun-founded-2023 stands. (2) StepFun's own newsroom post dated 26 January 2026 ("官宣！印奇出任阶跃星辰董事长", in the build file) states that Yin Qi took up the post of chairman and names CEO Jiang Daxin, chief scientist Zhang Xiangyu and CTO Zhu Yibo as the core management team: it is a primary expression of the four existing reporting-based role records, which were not redrafted; the coordinator may add it as evidence to them. The same post says Yin Qi is also chairman of 千里科技 (not drafted: no such entity in the dataset, and a statement by StepFun rather than by that company). (3) No StepFun or 01.AI funding announcement or filing was located; the StepFun B+ round rests on reporting and names no investors; earlier StepFun rounds and all 01.AI rounds were not drafted. (4) No standalone safety framework or commitment text published by StepFun or 01.AI was located on the pages searched; 01.AI's RAISE is recorded only as described in its two reports. (5) Left out for budget: StepFun's Step-Video-TI2V, GOT-OCR2.0, Step1X-3D, Step-Audio-EditX/R1, Step3-VL-10B, Step-3.7-Flash, StepAudio 3 and Step Edge releases and its partnership posts (Tencent Cloud, Kingdee, Qianli, 上海期智研究院); 01.AI's Yi-VL, Yi-9B and Yi-Large (API only, no document), the Yi licence v2.0 of 4 November 2023 (retrieved, not drafted), and the remaining timeline items of 01.AI's about page. (6) Authorship of existing StepFun people is recorded on the Step-3 report only; the same names appear on the Step-Video-T2V, Step-Audio, Step1X-Edit and NextStep-1 author lists. No existing dataset person appears in the Yi or Yi-Lightning author lists (Kai-Fu Lee is not listed). The Step1X-Edit PDF prints corresponding-author e-mail addresses; nothing from that passage is quoted.
- **iFlytek (科大讯飞) and SenseTime (商汤科技).** Searched HKEXnews (title search for SenseTime, stock 00020, 2021–2026: the 7 December 2021 Global Offering prospectus, the 2022, 2023 and 2025 annual reports, the 17 December 2023 announcement, the 24 April 2024 SenseNova 5.0 announcement and the 26 June 2025 AGM results announcement), sensetime.com (news pages are a Next.js site whose old news-detail URLs return HTTP 404, including https://www.sensetime.com/cn/news-detail/51167729?categoryId=72 linked from the SenseNova 5.0 filing and https://www.sensetime.com/cn/news-detail/51169523?categoryId=72 from the site’s own sitemap.txt; https://www.sensetime.com/cn/news-index and /en/news-index 404; the current /cn/news listing pages client-side, so the April 2023 SenseNova launch post was not reached; the /cn/ethics page and its linked oss.sensetime.com PDFs were retrieved), the sensenova and SenseTime Hugging Face accounts (model listing; SenseNova-U1-8B-MoT and SenseNova-SI-1.1-InternVL3-8B commit lists; the U1 LICENSE at commit 3b06420 returned HTTP 404), cninfo (announcement search for iFlytek, 002230: annual reports 2012–2025, the 2008 IPO prospectus, Spark launch notices of May 2023, August 2023 and June 2024, the 2025 sustainability report and the January 2026 board resolutions; static.cninfo.com.cn often reset the first connection and the tool retried), iflytek.com (https://www.iflytek.com/cn/ is a JavaScript shell, HTTP 200 with about 3 KB; https://www.iflytek.com/news.html HTTP 404; the English site was not used), and the iflytekopensource/iFlytekSpark-13B Gitee repository (blobless clone for history; raw files at pinned commits, after connection resets). The Hugging Face API returned no iflytek-authored models. Left out: SenseTime’s supplemental prospectus of 20 December 2021 and its cornerstone investors (conditional agreements); round-level amounts and valuations; other principal Pre-IPO Investors (Primavera, Silver Lake, CDH, Sailing, Shanghai International Group, China Structural Reform Fund) and the Taobao China Holding Limited holding (the prospectus passage read does not tie it to Alibaba Group); co-founders Lin Dahua, Ma Kun and Xu Chiheng; SenseTime’s earlier ethics publications (2020 Code of Ethics white paper, 2021–2022 reports) and its 2024 trustworthiness report; SenseNova-SI and other SenseNova open-weight repositories; iFlytek Spark V3.0, V3.5 and X1 release announcements (not located on cninfo; X1 recorded only through the 2025 annual report), the date of the change of iFlytek’s registered name, the 2025–2026 non-public offering, officers other than the chairman and president, and the Spark-13B LICENSE_MODEL.md texts of 30 January and 2 February 2024. No iFlytek-published AI ethics principles or frontier safety framework was located. The SenseTime 2022 annual report was retrieved but not cited (it does not mention SenseNova). Web.archive.org was not tried (refuses connections here). Authorship: no authored records were drafted; the white paper names editors and authors who are not otherwise in the dataset.
- **Tencent (腾讯) and Hunyuan (混元).** Searched on 29 October 2026, direct retrieval only. arXiv API title searches for Hunyuan, HunyuanVideo, Hunyuan3D, HunyuanImage, HunyuanWorld, HunyuanOCR and related titles (about 27 reports found; five cited at version 1: Hunyuan-DiT 2405.08748, Hunyuan-Large 2411.02265, Hunyuan-TurboS 2505.15431, HunyuanImage 3.0 2509.23951, Hunyuan-A13B 2609.27284. Retrieved but not drafted: HunyuanVideo 2412.03603 and Hunyuan3D 2.0 2501.12202, whose title pages name only the 'Hunyuan Foundation Model Team' and 'Hunyuan3D Team' with no corporate affiliation (HunyuanVideo is recorded from its tencent Hugging Face model card instead), and Hunyuan-MT 2509.05209, left out for budget). Hugging Face API for the tencent organisation (100 most recent repositories with licence tags) and model info, commit lists, LICENSE and README files at head revisions for Tencent-Hunyuan-Large, HunyuanVideo, Hunyuan-A13B-Instruct, Hy3-preview, Hy3 and Hy4-preview; the Hugging Face commit list endpoint returned only one page of commits, so head revisions were taken from the model-info API and earlier licence revisions were not compared. tencent.com: financial-reports page, Annual Reports 2025 and 2023 (English PDFs from static.www.tencent.com, not the HKEXnews copies), the reports page and ESG Report 2025, the governance page, and site searches for 'Hunyuan' and 'AI safety', which located the Hunyuan launch post (7 September 2023) and the Hy3 preview, Hy3 and Hy4 preview posts. Refusal: the WordPress API at https://www.tencent.com/wp-json/wp/v2/posts?search=Hunyuan returned HTTP 401 (the site search page was used instead). Not retrieved: HKEXnews filings (not searched; the tencent.com copies were used), GitHub Tencent-Hunyuan repositories (not needed for the licence texts, which were read on Hugging Face), the Chinese-language versions of the newsroom posts and annual reports, and the Internet Archive (refused from this environment). Left out for budget, though sourced in the cache: Dowson Tong's role as Senior Executive Vice President and CEO of CSIG in the 2023 launch post; the Hong Kong governing-law clause of the licences; principle 4 of the Responsible AI Principles (communicating capability limits). Left out: individual shareholdings in Tencent Computer stated in the annual report (living persons' shareholdings); performance and benchmark statements; licences of the many other tencent repositories (Hy-MT2, Hy-Memory and others show Apache-2.0 tags; HunyuanImage-3.0 and HY-World-2.0 show 'other'), which were seen only as API tags; Hy4 preview's Apache-2.0 LICENSE (retrieved, noted in the Hy4 preview record's qualification only); authorship records (no person in existing-entities appears in the author lists; Yao Shunyu appears in the A13B report only as a cited author); founding (no Tencent founding statement retrieved; the annual reports give Tencent Technology's establishment date but not the holding company's incorporation date). No Tencent AI safety framework in the sense of a standalone published document was located; the Responsible AI Principles in the ESG Report 2025 are recorded instead. Doubtful identity: the existing entity `tencent` carries the alias 深圳市腾讯计算机系统有限公司, which the annual report (in English) describes as a separate operating company controlled by contract; this draft proposes a separate entity for it, and the alias needs a decision.
- **BAAI (北京智源人工智能研究院) and Shanghai AI Laboratory (上海人工智能实验室).** Retrieved 29 October 2026, sequentially, direct route only. BAAI: www.baai.ac.cn is now a single-page application; its home page and the former pages www.baai.ac.cn/news/beijing-ai-principles.html, www.baai.ac.cn/blog/beijing-ai-principles and www.baai.ac.cn/portal/article/index/type/center_result/id/110.html (the Beijing AI Principles) all return HTTP 200 with the same empty JavaScript shell, so the site's text was read from its script file (assets/index-hCeadbk4.js) and one page of its news API (/api/news, whose items link to WeChat posts and carry re-import dates, so it was not used as evidence). BAAI Community (hub.baai.ac.cn) posts 4041 (second anniversary), 8375 (Wu Dao 2.0, 2021 conference), 2197 (Principles for Children) and 27398 (Wu Dao 3.0, 2023 conference; a repost of a media article, not used) were read. 2021.baai.ac.cn has no Wu Dao text. The Chinese text of the Beijing AI Principles (2019) was not found on any BAAI page; www.linking-ai-principles.org/cnprinciples failed (expired TLS certificate, then connection reset; no HTTP status); the English text was taken from ai-ethics-and-governance.institute, and the co-publishers from China Daily (reporting); Beijing News (bjnews.com.cn, 26 May 2019) was read but gives only a summary. No statement of BAAI's registered legal form (e.g. 民办非企业单位) was found; a web search found none, and registry aggregators were not used. BAAI's hosting of the International Dialogues on AI Safety (its timeline: 北京AI安全国际对话举办, March 2024; idais-beijing.baai.ac.cn) was not recorded: hosting is not authorship and the statement was not drafted. Wu Dao 1.0 (20 March 2021) has no record of its own because only the 2021 conference post mentions it; Wu Dao 3.0 and later BAAI models (Emu3.5, the 悟界 series, RoboBrain) were left out for budget. Hugging Face: commit lists and READMEs for BAAI/Aquila-7B (three pages of commits; earliest 8 June 2023), BAAI/Emu3-Gen and BAAI/bge-large-zh-v1.5; arXiv PDFs and abstract pages for 2307.05222, 2409.18869, 2309.07597 and 2408.07410 (v1). Authorship records for Huang Tiejun (Emu, 2307.05222) and Wang Zhongyuan were not drafted, since neither is in existing-entities. Shanghai AI Laboratory: www.shlab.org.cn home page (first connection reset, then HTTP 200) and /aboutus (gives no founding date; describes itself as 新型科研机构); no founding date was found on a laboratory or government page, so the unveiling of 11 July 2020 rests on Yicai; chinanews.com.cn/gn/2021/05-14/9476944.shtml (HTTP 200) did not yield the article body in the text layer and was not used; two touch.shio.gov.cn pages were unrelated. No laboratory or filing text naming its legal entity (上海人工智能创新中心) was found; the CAC lists are left to the governance group. GitHub InternLM/InternLM was read through a blobless clone for commit history and raw.githubusercontent.com for the initial README; Hugging Face commit lists and files for internlm/internlm2-chat-7b (README first created 15 January 2024; the initial weights commit has no README, HTTP 404), internlm/Intern-S1 and OpenGVLab/InternVL-Chat-V1-5 (commit list only; its licence was not drafted); arXiv PDFs and abstract pages for 2403.17297, 2312.14238, 2508.15763, 2507.18576 and 2507.16534 (v1). Zhou Bowen's authorship is recorded only where the report names him as Scientific Director; his place in the long Intern-S1 author list was not drafted. Existing records reused: Zhou Bowen's role from April 2024 and the Frontier AI Risk Management Framework v1.0 records. web.archive.org was not used.
- **Governance texts: CAC listings and TC260 links.** Retrieved 2026-10-29, sequentially and directly (no relay, no archive). CAC: the announcement page of 2 April 2024 (cac.gov.cn/2024-04/02/c_1713729983803145.htm), which on retrieval listed 13 attachments (附件1 生成式人工智能服务已备案信息（2024年4月） to 附件13 …（2026年8月）); all 13 PDFs were fetched through the page's downloadfile.jsp links (one connection reset on 附件5, retried successfully) and their rows were counted from pdftotext layout output; five listings are proposed as documents (2024-04, 2024-08, 2025-01, 2025-08, 2026-08: the opening list, the earliest with a registered section, the one reaching the stated end-2024 total, the earliest with a BAAI entry, and the latest). Cross-checks: the filed numbering reaches 302 in the January 2025 list and 748 in the December 2025 list, and the registered numbering 105 and 435, matching the totals the CAC states for 31 December 2024 and 31 December 2025. The CAC's announcement of 9 January 2026 on 2025 filings (cac.gov.cn/2026-01/09/c_1769688009588554.htm) was retrieved after two connection resets. Deep-synthesis algorithm filings: the CAC announcements of 20 June 2023 (first) and 17 July 2026 (eighteenth batch, the latest located) and their attachments, which are .docx files; the pack tool stores no text layer for them, so their counts were made from word/document.xml and are carried as paraphrase on the announcement pages. Batches 2 to 17 were not retrieved (bounded). Covered developers were searched in all 13 generative-AI attachments by name: StepFun, 01.AI, iFlytek, SenseTime, Tencent and Shanghai AI Laboratory appear in the April 2024 list, BAAI (北京智源人工智能研究院) earliest in the August 2025 list; of the earlier collections' developers, Zhipu, MiniMax and Baidu (April 2024) and DeepSeek (August 2024) were recorded because the dataset already holds their listed legal names; Moonshot AI (北京月之暗面科技有限公司, April 2024 list, row 16), ByteDance (北京抖音信息服务有限公司, 云雀大模型, row 3) and Alibaba (阿里巴巴达摩院（杭州）科技有限公司, row 11) appear but were left out because no dataset record or brief mapping ties those legal names to the dataset entities. Latest-listing entries per developer were not drafted (budget). In the first deep-synthesis list iFlytek (科大讯飞股份有限公司), Tencent (深圳市腾讯计算机系统有限公司), Zhipu and Baidu entities appear; not drafted. Provincial lists of locally filed services and the CAC algorithm-filing system (beian.cac.gov.cn) were not searched. TC260: the three framework editions were fetched again from the URLs of the dataset sources (SHA-256 identical to the dataset's records) and searched for provisions not yet recorded, especially on developers and open-source models; the CAC news item for 2.0 (cac.gov.cn/2025-09/15/c_1759653448369123.htm) and TC260's news item for 3.0 were read for replacement wording: neither news item says an edition replaces another; the 2.0 and 3.0 prefaces say each was drafted on the basis of the earlier editions and updates them, recorded as two supersedes_document candidates for reviewer decision. No retrieval was refused (no HTTP 4xx/5xx); cac.gov.cn reset first connections several times (curl error 35), each retried successfully. Web search was used for discovery of the 2026 and deep-synthesis announcement URLs only.
